Volume - 158 Issue 08 BENGALURU, TUESDAY, 10, JANUARY, 2023(PUSHYA, 20, SHAKAVARSHA, 1944)

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 153 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:04-01-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 153 PFH 2022
Dated:10-08-2022 regarding altering the limits of Murudi village of Yelburga Taluk
and Koppal District and constituting a new revenue village called Narayanapura was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka
act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 153 PFH 2022 Dated: 19-08-
2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:27-10-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the State
Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Murudi village of Yelburga taluk in Koppal District to form a new revenue village
(1259)

ANDO BMF UE TWD, Dorsad, OO, BHO, 509% eon Yo
called Narayanapura of Yelburga taluk Koppal district along with survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 241 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:04-01-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village and
other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Udabal
of Chitaguppa taluk Bidar District and to constitute a new revenue village called
Udabal wadi as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and
new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner, Bidar District, Bidar.

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Udabal of Chitguppa taluk in Bidar District to form a new revenue village
called Udabal wadi of Chitguppa taluk Bidar district along with the survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

PR-61
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 394 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:04-01-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act,
1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors specified in
clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such as the
population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Sevanagr Village of Shahabad Taluk of Kalaburagi District to convert the existing

Bhankur wada (A) & (B) unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extension /part of the
existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is specified
in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Sevanagr village for conversion of Bhankur wada(A)&(B) unrecorded habitation in
to hamlet/extension/ part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period
specified above will be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of Kalaburagi District.

SCHEDULE 1
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Bhankur wada (A) village of Shahabad
Taluk of Kalaburagi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the
boundary of Sevanagr Revenue village and same will herein after be named as Bhankur
wada (A) (Upanagar) of Sevanagar village in Shahabad Taluk of Kalaburagi District.

survey numbers specified in column (3) on which the said unrecorded habitation is
located is proposed to be converted as habitation area ( Gramatana) of Bhankur wada
(A) & (B) hamlet/ extension/part of Sevanagar village in Shahabad Taluk of
Kalaburagi District.

The proposal specified above Shall come in to effect from the date of final
notification to be published in the official Gazette by the State Government under
section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964).

PR-62
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 245 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:04-01-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience the Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the
limits of Pandaragera village of Basavakalyan taluk in Bidar district and to constitute
a new village called Ghanalal as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey
and new numbers assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Bidar district, Bidar.

SCHEDULE-1

Lands bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Pandaragera of Basavakalyan taluk in Bidar district to form a new revenue
village called Ghanalal of Basavakalyan taluk in Bidar district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 244 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:04-01-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience the Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the

limits of Ladawanti village of Basavakalyan taluk in Bidar district and to constitute
a new village called Ladawanti Tanda as specified in schedule along with maps with
old survey and new numbers assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Bidar district, Bidar.

SCHEDULE-1

Lands bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Ladawanti of Basavakalyan taluk in Bidar district to form a new revenue
village called Ladawanti Tanda of Basavakalyan taluk in Bidar district along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 243 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Stored Building
Bengaluru Date:04-01-2023
PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka land Revenue
Act,1964(Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such

as the population, Area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience .The Government of Karnataka proposes issue a notification to alter the
limits of Kurub Khelgi Village of Bhalki taluk, Bidar district to convert the
existing Bhagyanagar into new revenue village as specified in Schedule.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the state government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner, Bidar District Bidar.

SCHEDULE-I
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in coloumn(2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Kurub Khelgi of Bhalki taluk in Bidar District to form a new revenue village
called as Bhagyanagar of Bhalki taluk and Bidar District along with the survey
numbers included in coloum (4) there of.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka land Revenue
Act,1964(Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such as
the population, Area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience. The Government of Karnataka proposes issue a notification to alter the
limits of Handikera village of Kamalnagar Taluk, Bidar district to constitute a new
village called Handikera Tanda as specified in Schedule along with maps with old
survey and new survey numbers assigned to them.

Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of tall the persons likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken consideration on or after thirty days from the date of
publication of the Notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government from
any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner Bidar District Bidar.
SCHEDULE-I
Lands bearing Survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Handikera of Kamalnagar Taluk in Bidar District to from new revenue
village called as Handikera Tanda of Kamalnagar Taluk and Bidar District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-66

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 246 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:04-01-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act,
1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors specified in clauses
(i) to (iv} of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area
under land revenue and having regard to the administrative convenience, the Government of
Karnataka proposes to after, the extent of residential area by adding an area occupied by
unrecorded habitation within the boundary of Gola(K) Village of Shahabad Taluk of
Kalaburagi District to convert the existing Ramnagar Tanda Nizam Bajar unrecorded
habitation in to a hamlet/extension /part of the existing revenue village. The area occupied by
the said hamlet of the village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified in
Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of Gola(K) village for
conversion of Ramnagar Tanda Nizam Bajar unrecorded habitation in to hamlet/extension/
part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act, notice is
hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby that the proposal
will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification
in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government from any
persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period specified above will
be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner of Kalaburagi District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the table shall
be declared as habitation area of Ramnagar Tanda Nizam Bajar village of Shahabad Taluk
of Kalaburagi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of
Gola(K) Revenue village and same will herein after be named as Ramnagar Tanda Nizam
Bajar of Gola(K) village in Shahabad Taluk of Kalaburagi District.

factors specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, Area under land revenue and having regard to the
administrative convenience .The Government of Karnataka proposes issue a
notification to alter the limits of Eklar village of Aurad (B) Taluk, Bidar district to
convert the existing Eklar Tanda into new revenue village as specified in Schedule.

Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of tall the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken consideration on or after thirty days from the
date of publication of the Notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of period specified
above will be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestions may be
addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Bidar District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act
1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors specified
in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such as the
population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village and other

head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Chengta
Village of Kamlapur Taluk kalburgi District and to constitute a new revenue village
called Mallikoll nagar, Kadadari nagar, jammankolli nagar, Motiram nagar,
Adakimoke nagar, Honbat nagar as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act, notice is
hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected thereby that the
proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Kalburgi District, Kalburgi

specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Chengta of Kamlapur Taluk in Kalburgi District to form a new revenue village
called Mallikolla nagar of Kamlapur Taluk Kalburgi district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-69

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 393 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:04-01-2023
PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors specified in clauses (i) to
(iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area under
land revenue and having regard to the administrative convenience, the Government of
Karnataka proposes to after, the extent of residential area by adding an area occupied by
unrecorded habitation within the boundary of Bhankur Village of Shahabad Taluk of
Kalaburagi District to convert the existing Bhankur Banjara colony unrecorded habitation
in to a hamlet/extension /part of the existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said
hamlet of the village is specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified in
Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of Bhankur village for
conversion of Bhankur Banjara colony unrecorded habitation in to hamlet/extension/ part of
the said revenue village.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act, notice is
hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby that the proposal
will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification
in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government from any
persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period specified above will
be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner of Kalaburagi District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the table shall
be declared as habitation area of Bhankur Banjara colony village of Shahabad Taluk of
Kalaburagi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of
Bhankur Revenue village and same will herein after be named as Bhankur Banjara colony
of Bhankur village in Shahabad Taluk of Kalaburagi District.

It is hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the columns(4) above with
survey numbers specified in column (3) on which the said unrecorded habitation is
located is proposed to be converted as habitation area ( Gramatana) of Bhankur
Banjara colony hamlet/ extension/part of Bhankur village in Shahabad Taluk of
Kalaburagi District.

The proposal specified above Shall come in to effect from the date of final
notification to be published in the official Gazette by the State Government under
section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964).

Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:04-01-2023

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience the Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the
limits of Betagera village of Basavakalyan taluk in Bidar district and to constitute a
newvillage called Lakshminagar as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey and new numbers assigned to them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Bidar district, Bidar.
SCHEDULE-1

Lands bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Betagera of Basavakalyan taluk in Bidar district to form a new revenue village
called Lakshminagar of Basavakalyan taluk in Bidar district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the power conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act,
1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors specified
in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such as the
population, area of the village, the demand under land revenue in the village and Other
Head Of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience, the
government of Karnataka proposes to issue a notification to alter the limits of Anabi

village of Shahapur Taluk Yadgir District and to constitute a new village called
Hosuru as specified in schedule along with Maps with Old Survey Numbers and new
assigned to them.
Now therefore, in exercise of the Powers conferred by section 6 of The said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from the date of
publication of the Notification in the official gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government from
any person with respect to the said Proposal before the date specified above will be
considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner, Yadgir District, Yadgir.

SCHEDULE-I

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Anabi of Shahapur Taluk in Yadgir district to form the a new revenue village
called Hosuru of Shahapur Taluk, Yadgir district along with the survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

RD 154 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:06-01-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification RD 154 PFH 2022 Dated: 16-08-2022
regarding altering the limits of Ballutagi village of YelburgaTaluk and Koppal District
and constituting a new revenue village called BhagyaNagar was published as required
by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964)
in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 154 PFH 2022 dated:20-8-2022 inviting objections
or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated :27-10-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the State
Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Ballutagi village of Yelburga taluk in Koppal District to form a new revenue village
called BhagyaNagar of Yelburga taluk Koppal district along with survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 82 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Stored Building
Bengaluru Date:02-01-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 82 PFH 2022
Dated:04-07-2022 regarding altering the limits of Saigaon of Bhalki Talak in Bidar
District and constituting a new revenue village called Basavanawadi and Pandri was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka
act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 82 PFH 2022 dated:22-07-2022
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:16-08-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-I
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in coloumn(2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Saigaon of Bhalki Talak in Bidar District to form a new revenue village called
as Basavana wadi of Bhalki taluk and Bidar District along with the survey numbers
included in coloum (4) there of.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 348 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:02-01-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 348 PFH 2022 Dated:08-
11-2022 regarding altering the limits of Panegaon Village of Kalburagi taluk
Kalburagi District and constituting a new revenue village called Shivaji Tanda and
Basavan Tanda was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 348 PFH
2022 dated:09-11-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to
be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:10-
11-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Shivaji Tanda (sub) village of Panegaon
Taluk of Kalaburagi District convert the same to form hamlet/extension/sub village

within the boundary of Panegaon Revenue village and same will here in after be named
as Shivaji Tanda (Newly created) Panegaon of village in Kalaburagi Taluk of
Kalaburagi District.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 93 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Stored Building
Bengaluru Date:02-01-2023
FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 93 GLH 2022
Dated:04-07-2022 regarding altering the limits of Mehakar village of Bhalki Taluk,
Bidar District and constituting a new revenue village called Balaji nagar was published
as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 93 GLH 2022 dated:22-07-2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty
days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:16-08-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-I
Lands bearing Survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Mehakar village of Bhalki taluk, Bidar District to from new revenue village
called as Balaji nagar of Bhalki taluk and Bidar District along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 80 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Stored Building
Bengaluru Date:02-01-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 80 PFH 2022
Dated:05-07-2022 regarding altering the limits of Maskal village of Aurad (B) Taluk,
Bidar District and constituting a new revenue village called Maskal Tanda was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka
act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 80 PFH 2022 dated:25-07-2022
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:16-10-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-I

Lands bearing Survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Maskal of Aurad(B) Taluk in Bidar District to from new revenue village
called as Maskal Tanda of Aurad(B) Taluk and Bidar District along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 233 PFH 2022
Dated: 11-10-2022 regarding altering the limits of Kanchagarahalli Village of
Yadagir taluk Yadagir District and constituting a new revenue village called
Gojimatanagar was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 233 PFH
2022 dated:14-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to
be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
dated:21-10-2022

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Kanchagarahalli of Yadagir taluk in Yadagir District to form a new revenue
village called Gojimatanagar of Yadagir taluk Yadagir district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 283 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:02-01-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 283 PFH 2022
Dated:10-10-2022 regarding altering the limits of Jinakera village of Yadagiri Taluk
and Yadagiri District and constituting a new revenue village called Govindnagar was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka
act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 283 PFH 2022 dated:12-10-2022
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:21-10-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Jinakera of Yadagiri taluk in Yadagiri District to form a new revenue village
called Govindanagar of Yadagiri taluk Yadagiri district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 110 PFH 2022
Dated:24-06-2022 regarding altering the limits of Hullyal village of Aurad (B) Taluk,
Bidar District and constituting a new revenue village called Laskar Naik Tanda was

published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka
act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 110 PFH 2022 dated:01-08-2022
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:16-10-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-I

Lands bearing Survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages of Hullyal of Aurad(B) Taluk in Bidar District to from new revenue village
called as Laskar Naik Tanda of Aurad(B) Taluk and Bidar district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 218 PFH 2022
Dated: 11-10-2022 regarding altering the limits of Hagaratagi village of Hunsagi
Taluk and Yadgir District and constituting a new revenue village called Horatti was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka
act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 218 PFH 2022 dated:13-10-2022
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
dated:21-10-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:
SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Hagaratagi of Hunsagi taluk in Yadagiri District to form a new revenue village
called Horatti of Hunsagi taluk Yadagiri district along with the survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

PR-86
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 223 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:02-01-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 223 GLH 2022
Dated:08-11-2022 regarding altering the limits of Hagarga Village of Kalaburagi Taluk of
Kalaburagi District to convert the existing Manunayak Hagarga Tanda unrecorded habitation in to
a hamlet/extension /part of the existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the
village was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka
act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 223 GLH 2022 dated:09-11-2022 inviting

objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the
date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 10-11-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the State
Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here by makes the following
notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the table shall be
declared as habitation area of Hagarga village of Kalaburagi Taluk of Kalaburagi District convert
the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of Hagarga Revenue village and same will
herein after be named as Hagarga Tanda of Hagarga village in Kalaburagi Taluk of Kalaburagi
District.

It is hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the columns(4) above with survey
numbers specified in column (3) on which the said unrecorded habitation is located is proposed to be
converted as habitation area( Gramatana) of Hagarga tanda hamlet/ extension/part of Hagarga
village in Kalaburagi Taluk of Kalaburagi District.

The above shall come into effect from date of publication of final notification in the official
gazette.

By order in the name of the
Governor of Karnataka

(Dr. Rajendra Prasad M. N.,)
Director,

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 311 PFH 2022
Dated:05-11-2022 regarding altering the limits of Jambagi village of Aurad(B)
Taluk, Bidar District and constituting a new revenue village called Ghama Tanda was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka
act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 311 PFH 2022
dated:07-11-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:05-12-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-I

Lands bearing Survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Jambagi of Aurad(B) Taluk in Bidar District to from new revenue village

called as Ghama Tanda of Aurad(B) Taluk and Bidar District along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 353 PFH 2022
Dated:07-11-2022 regarding altering the limits of Elenavadagi village of Aland Taluk
and Kalburagi District and constituting a new revenue village called Navadagi thanda
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 353 PFH 2022
dated:08-11-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:08-11-2022

And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of
Elenavadagi said village of Aland taluk in Kalburagi District to form a new revenue
village called Navadagi thanda of Aland taluk Kalburagi district along with survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 222 GLH 2022
Dated:07-11-2022 regarding altering the limits of Dorjambaga village of Kamalapur
Taluk and Kalburagi District and constituting a new revenue village called
Dorjambaga Nagar was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD
222 GLH 2022 dated 08-11-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:09-
11-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of
said village Dorjambaga of Kamalapur taluk in Kalaburagi District to form a
new revenue village called Dorajambaga Nagar of Kamalapur taluk Kalaburagi
district along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 94 GLH 2022
Dated:04-07-2022 regarding altering the limits of Mudhol(B) of Kamalanagar Taluk
in Bidar District and constituting a new revenue village called Dhansing Tanda was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka
act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 94 GLH 2022 dated:22-07-2022
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:21-10-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-I

Lands bearing Survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Mudhol(B) of Kamalanagar Taluk in Bidar District to from new revenue
village called as Dhansing Tanda of Kamalanagar Taluk and Bidar District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby

here by makes the following notification, namely:-

villages Bonti of Aurad(B) Taluk in Bidar District to from new revenue village called

numbers included in column (4) there of.

to a hamlet/extension /part of the existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the
village was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka
act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 201 GLH 2022 dated:07-11-2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the
date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here by makes the following
notification, namely:-

convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of Bhimanal Revenue village and
same will herein after be named as Manunayak nagar of Bhimanal village in Kamalapur Taluk of

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 135 GLH 2022
Dated:10-08-2022 regarding altering the limits of Bagadala Village of Bidar taluk
Bidar District and constituting a new revenue village called Indiranagara was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka
act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 135 GLH 2022 dated:17-08-2022
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:15-11-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village BagadalaVillage of Bidar taluk in Bidar District to form a new revenue village
called Indiranagara of Bidar taluk Bidar district along with the survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 253 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:02-01-2023

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 253 PFH 2022
Dated: 11-10-2022 regarding altering the limits of Totloor Village of Gurumatkal
taluk Yadgir District and constituting a new revenue village called Sevaghad Tanda
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 253 PFH 2022
dated:12-10-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated. 12-10-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Totloor of Gurumatkal taluk in Yadiri District to form a new revenue village
called Sevaghad Tanda of Gurumatkal taluk Yadgir district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.
TABLE-1

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 236 PFH 2022 Dated: 10-11-2022
regarding altering the limits of Magdampur Village of Guramatakal taluk Yadgir
District and constituting a new revenue village called Somulalnagar was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 236 PFH 2022 dated:12-10-2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty
days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated. 12-10-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Magdampur of Guramatakal taluk in Yadgir District to form a new revenue
village called Somulalnagar of Guramatakal taluk Yadgir district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 239 PFH 2022 Dated:. 10-10-2022
regarding altering the limits of Baddepalli Village of Gurumatkal taluk Yadgiri
District and constituting a new revenue village called Jai Shriramanagar was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka
act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 239 PFH 2022 dated:12-10-2022
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated. 12-10-2022.
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas specified
in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said village
Baddepalli of Gurumatkal taluk in Yadgiri District to form a new revenue village
called Jai Shriramanagar of Gurumatkal taluk Yadgiri istrict along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.
